Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The organelles variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 255 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 756 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the organelles space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 255 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 756. QED.
